Christmas Present Quest 2020

## 64

The 75th floor boss is The Skull Reaper.   
A single swing of it's large scythe has   
the power to completely wipe out a player's HP.   
After killing over 14 players in battle,   
it became apparent to the assault team   
just how difficult clearing the game is.

## 65

bg\_thumbnail\_2029

## 66

SAO Boss Battle Challenge (Master +1)

## 67

The 74th floor boss is The Gleam Eyes.   
A demon possessing high attack power   
and a sturdy defense.   
Thanks to his unique skill "Dual Blades",   
Kirito just barely made it out of the battle alive.

## 68

SAO Boss Battle Challenge (Master)

## 69

The 55th floor, X'rphan the White Wyrm   
from the West Mountain.   
In order to obtain metal for his second sword,   
Kirito ventures to the home of the white wyrm   
in the mountains with Lisbeth.   
While their strategy didn’t go as  planned,   
they successfully obtained what they came for.

## 70

bg\_thumbnail\_2018

## 71

SAO Boss Battle Challenge (EX)

## 72

The 35th floor event boss is Nicholas the Renegade.   
An  event boss that appears under a fir tree on the 24th   
of December late at night.   
In order to obtain resurrection items,   
Kirito left to challenge the foe himself.

## 73

SAO Boss Battle Challenge (Expert)

## 74

The 1st floor boss is Illfang the Kobold Lord.   
It proved to be an even more challenging and   
tougher foe in comparison to its strength during   
the game's beta testing.   
Carrying out Diavel’s dying wish,   
Kirito and the others   
magnificently claim victory over the Kobold Lord.

Can you smash all 70 watermelons before time runs out?  
But be careful! MP won't recover much in this quest!  
Fireworks will appear after every 10 watermelons smashed.

You might even spot the rare Golden Watermelon!

## 126

You've reached the final stage of “The Art of Charging MP”!  
Complete this quest and master the skill!  
"After a successful parry,   
immediately activate your skill and strike the enemy   
with a 10+ hit combo" three times to clear!  
Use an SS3 with multiple hits!

## 129

The Art of Charging MP: Mastery

## 130

Practice what you learned in battle!  
In this quest,   
MP will completely vanish after a certain amount of time!  
Charge MP with normal attacks while   
jumping to avoid the boss's attacks!  
After a successful parry,   
immediately activate your skill and strike the enemy   
with a 10+ hit combo to clear!  
Use an SS3 with multiple hits!

## 131

Immediately after a parry, land a 10+ hit combo with a skill.

## 133

The Art of Charging MP: Practice 3

## 134

Practice what you learned in battle!  
In this quest,   
MP will completely vanish after a certain amount of time!  
Wait for the moment the boss lands   
and charge MP with normal attacks!  
After a successful parry,   
immediately activate your skill and strike the enemy   
with a 10+ hit combo to clear!  
Use an SS3 with multiple hits!

## 135

The Art of Charging MP: Practice 2

## 136

Practice what you learned in battle!  
In this quest,   
MP will completely vanish after a certain amount of time!  
Circle behind the enemy while   
it's attacking and charge MP with normal attacks!  
After a successful parry,   
immediately activate your skill and strike the enemy   
with a 10+ hit combo to clear!  
Use an SS3 with multiple hits!

## 137

The Art of Charging MP: Practice 1

## 138

Yuuki will teach you how to charge MP!  
In this quest,   
MP will completely vanish after a certain amount of time!  
Charge MP with normal attacks!  
After a successful parry,   
immediately activate your skill and strike the enemy   
with a 10+ hit combo to clear!  
Use an SS3 with multiple hits!

Complete this quest and master the skill!  
Successfully parry six times to clear!

## 142

The Art of Parrying: Mastery

## 143

Practice what you learned in battle!  
Dodging the boss's attacks is a bit more difficult this time!  
Listen to Kirito's advice and jump to avoid powerful attacks!   
After that, aim for a parry!   
Successfully parry three times to clear!

## 144

Parry the enemy three times.

Practice what you learned in battle!

Successfully parry three times to clear!
